<script type="text/javascript">
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
    const banner = document.querySelector('.stickyBanner');
    const closeButton = document.getElementById('closeStickyBanner');

    if (closeButton) {
        closeButton.addEventListener('click', function() {
            let cookie = "stickyBannerClosed=true; path=/; max-age=" + 60 * 60 * 24 * 30;

            if (banner) {
                banner.classList.add('hidden');
                document.cookie = cookie;

                setTimeout(() => {
                    banner.style.display = 'none';
                }, 400);

                const isMobile = window.matchMedia("(max-width: 992px)").matches;
                if (isMobile) {
                    window.scrollBy({
                        top: 1,
                        behavior: 'smooth'
                    });
                }
            }
        });
    }

    if (banner) {
        window.addEventListener('scroll', () => {
            if (window.scrollY > 150) {
                banner.classList.add('hidden');
            } else {
                banner.classList.remove('hidden');
            }
        });
    }
});
</script><div class="container">

                                80% cut in approval time per proposal                            </h3>

    <section class="textBlock textBlock--section " id="">
        <div class="container">
            <div class="row">
                <div class=" col-12  textBlock__content ">
                    <h2 class="ai-optimize-6 ai-optimize-introduction">Company</h2>
<p class="ai-optimize-8">Banco do Brasil and Votorantim combined in 1988 to form Banco BV, one of Brazil’s largest<br>
private banks. The multi-product digital bank offers innovative solutions: accounts, cards, loans and financing for individuals, insurance and assistance, institutional investors, vehicles, Corporate &amp; Investment Banking, Asset Management and Private Banking.</p>
<p>&nbsp;</p>
<h2 class="ai-optimize-9">Context</h2>
<p class="ai-optimize-10">With strong roots in vehicle financing and a market leader in the solar panel loans segment, Banco BV has transformed itself into a cutting edge digital bank. While this opened up opportunities for the institution, it also meant that the bank needed knowledge, skills and, specifically, technological expertise to deliver this new business model in an industry where fraud and scams are prevalent. The bank chose Feedzai to partner with them on their digital journey, implementing a multi-layered fraud prevention system. This helped Banco BV better understand its customers with a 360-degree view — from onboarding to real-time transactions — and deliver products to them safely and securely. As the seventh-largest bank in Brazil, headquartered in São Paulo, Banco BV continues to set a benchmark for innovation and customer trust in the digital banking space.</p>
<p>&nbsp;</p>
<h2 class="ai-optimize-6">The Challenge</h2>
<p class="ai-optimize-7">Banco BV faced the challenge of having a fragmented fraud prevention system that needed to be improved to match market maturity levels. The bank lacked a centralized and integrated approach to fraud prevention across its various products and channels.</p>
<p>&nbsp;</p>
<h2 class="ai-optimize-8">The Solution: Feedzai</h2>
<p class="ai-optimize-9">After extensive research and evaluation of vendors, Banco BV selected Feedzai as its partner, prioritizing cultural and technological alignment as well as the ability to address the bank’s needs, technical gaps, and fraud prevention maturity. Banco BV chose Feedzai for its comprehensive fraud prevention capabilities and its ability to integrate multiple monitoring processes into a single platform, providing a holistic view of customer transactions and activities.</p>
<p class="ai-optimize-9">Feedzai helped Banco BV by implementing a centralized fraud prevention system that included behavioral biometrics, transaction monitoring, and automation. This integration enabled Banco BV to effectively monitor financial and non-financial transactions, reduce fraud losses and improve customer experience. Threat intelligence helped the bank detect types of fraud such as account takeovers and the presence of malware on a customer’s device, allowing the bank to act quickly to stop malicious activity.</p>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    </section>


<section class="quote__section fullWidth halfBottomMargin halfTopMargin" id="">
    <div class="container">
        <div class="row">
            <div class="col-lg-8 offset-lg-2 quote__content">
                                                    <h2 class="quote__quote">"We have seen exceptional results by integrating Feedzai’s fraud prevention system. Our turnaround time on financing approvals has improved significantly and fraud losses have been drastically reduced, leading to a safer and more efficient banking experience for our customers."</h2>
                                                    <p class="quote__quoted">Cássia Dias, Head of Fraud Prevention, Banco BV</p>
                                            </div>
        </div>
    </div>
</section>
    <section class="textBlock textBlock--section " id="">
        <div class="container">
            <div class="row">
                <div class=" col-12  textBlock__content ">
                    <p class="ai-optimize-10">The catalyst for change was Banco BV’s vision to centralize risk operations across the bank, simplifying technology and moving from multiple solutions to using a single integrated platform — Feedzai. For initial use cases, Feedzai’s platform combined its Transaction Fraud for Banking (TFB) and Digital Trust solutions. The bank also had a special case related to new accounts, for which Feedzai developed an Account Opening solution.</p>
<p>&nbsp;</p>
<h2 class="ai-optimize-11">Implementation Process</h2>
<p class="ai-optimize-12">Banco BV started by integrating Feedzai’s TFB solution to monitor online transfers and transactions in its financing business. The success of this led the bank to integrate Feedzai’s Digital Trust solution to monitor customer interactions using behavioral biometrics.Banco BV continues to centralize its risk management operations: currently expanding its onboarding operations, as well as migrating card transaction monitoring to the Feedzai platform.</p>
<p>&nbsp;</p>
<h2 class="ai-optimize-13">Feedzai’s Key Capabilities</h2>
<ul>
<li class="ai-optimize-14"><strong>Account Opening</strong><br>
The flexibility of the Feedzai platform is enabling the bank to expand its onboarding operations by integrating ecosystem partners via API connectors, simplifying operations and reducing threats.</li>
<li class="ai-optimize-16"><strong>Transaction Monitoring</strong><br>
At the core of Feedzai’s RiskOps platform, real-time scoring of transactions across the customer lifecycle has driven efficiency through a 360-degree view, as well as accelerating the centralization of separate fraud prevention systems for Banco BV.</li>
<li class="ai-optimize-18"><strong>Behavioral Biometrics</strong><br>
Banco BV was able to better protect customers from fraud attacks using Feedzai’s account hacking module, which leverages device, network, malware and behavioral biometric information.</li>
</ul>
<p>&nbsp;</p>
<h2 class="ai-optimize-20">Results Achieved</h2>
<ul>
<li class="ai-optimize-21"><strong>Increase in Financing Approvals</strong><br>
Banco BV saw an exceptional change in results when Feedzai was integrated into the financing approval process.<br>
Service level agreement (SLA) reduced from 2 hours to 30 minutes, leading to more customer approvals.</li>
<li class="ai-optimize-23"><strong>Reducing False Positives in Financing Applications</strong><br>
The reduced false positive rate meant fewer rejections due to greater accuracy in identifying legitimate and<br>
fraudulent applications.</li>
<li class="ai-optimize-25"><strong>Expanding Fraud Prevention Across the Bank</strong><br>
Based on its success in financing solar panels, Banco BV is implementing Feedzai in existing and new<br>
products, as well as in other business areas.</li>
</ul>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    </section>

                                                                        <p class="contentSlice__text"></p><h2 class="ai-optimize-27 ai-optimize-introduction">Conclusion</h2>
<p class="ai-optimize-28">Banco BV’s foray into digital banking has not only increased options for new and existing customers in Brazil, but has also provided a safe and secure experience. By integrating Feedzai’s solution into its digital operations, Banco BV reduced false positives and increased approvals due to accurate decision-making. The bank continues to centralize risk operations across multiple business areas, with several initiatives already underway. These include extending the account opening solution to new bank accounts and credit card applications, expanding its onboarding operations, and migrating card transaction monitoring to the Feedzai platform.</p>

informações.</div></div></div><div id="onetrust-button-group-parent" class="ot-sdk-twelve ot-sdk-columns has-reject-all-button"><div id="onetrust-button-group"><button id="onetrust-pc-btn-handler" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true"><path class="ot-floating-button__svg-fill" d="M14.588 0l.445.328c1.807 1.303 3.961 2.533 6.461 3.688 2.015.93 4.576 1.746 id="ot-pc-title">Centro de preferências de privacidade</h2><div id="ot-pc-desc">Quando visita um website, este pode armazenar ou recolher informações no seu navegador, principalmente na forma de cookies. Esta informação pode ser sobre si, as suas preferências ou o seu dispositivo e é utilizada principalmente para fazer o website funcionar conforme o esperado. A informação normalmente não o identifica diretamente, mas pode dar-lhe uma experiência web mais personalizada. Uma vez que respeitamos o seu direito à privacidade, pode optar por não permitir alguns tipos de cookies. Clique nos cabeçalhos das diferentes categorias para saber mais e alterar as nossas configurações predefinidas. No entanto, o bloqueio de alguns tipos de cookies pode afetar a sua experiência no website e os serviços que podemos oferecer. Reveja as nossas <a href="https://www.feedzai.com/legal/feedzai-cookie-policy/">Cookies</a> e <a href="https://www.feedzai.com/legal/privacy-policy/">Privacy Policies</a> para mais informações.</div><button id="accept-recommended-btn-handler" ot-button-order="0">Permitir todos</button><section class="ot-sdk-row ot-cat-grp"><h3 id="ot-category-title">Gerir preferências de cookies</h3><div class="ot-accordion-layout ot-cat-item ot-vs-config" data-optanongroupid="C0001"><button ot-accordion="true" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="ot-desc-id-C0001" aria-labelledby="ot-header-id-C0001 ot-status-id-C0001"></button><!-- Accordion header --><div class="ot-acc-hdr ot-always-active-group"><div class="ot-plus-minus"><span></span><span></span></div><h4 class="ot-cat-header" id="ot-header-id-C0001">Cookies estritamente necessários</h4><div id="ot-status-id-C0001" class="ot-always-active">Sempre ativos</div></div><!-- accordion detail --><div class="ot-acc-grpcntr ot-acc-txt"><p class="ot-acc-grpdesc ot-category-desc" id="ot-desc-id-C0001">Estes cookies são necessários para que o website funcione e não podem ser desligados nos nossos sistemas. Normalmente, eles só são configurados em resposta a ações levadas a cabo por si e que correspondem a uma solicitação de serviços, tais como definir as suas preferências de privacidade, iniciar sessão ou preencher formulários. Pode configurar o seu navegador para bloquear ou alertá-lo(a) sobre esses cookies, mas algumas partes do website não funcionarão. Estes cookies não armazenam qualquer informação pessoal identificável.</p></div></div><div class="ot-accordion-layout ot-cat-item ot-vs-config" data-optanongroupid="C0004"><button ot-accordion="true" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="ot-desc-id-C0004" aria-labelledby="ot-header-id-C0004"></button><!-- Accordion header --><div class="ot-acc-hdr"><div class="ot-plus-minus"><span></span><span></span></div><h4 class="ot-cat-header" id="ot-header-id-C0004">Cookies de funcionalidade</h4><div class="ot-tgl"><input type="checkbox" name="ot-group-id-C0004" id="ot-group-id-C0004" role="switch" class="category-switch-handler" data-optanongroupid="C0004" checked="" aria-labelledby="ot-header-id-C0004" style=""> <label class="ot-switch" for="ot-group-id-C0004"><span class="ot-switch-nob"></span> <span class="ot-label-txt">Cookies de funcionalidade</span></label> </div></div><!-- accordion detail --><div class="ot-acc-grpcntr ot-acc-txt"><p class="ot-acc-grpdesc ot-category-desc" id="ot-desc-id-C0004">Estes cookies permitem que o site forneça uma funcionalidade e personalização melhoradas. Podem ser estabelecidos por nós ou por fornecedores externos cujos serviços adicionámos às nossas páginas. Se não permitir estes cookies algumas destas funcionalidades, ou mesmo todas, podem não atuar corretamente.</p></div></div><div class="ot-accordion-layout ot-cat-item ot-vs-config" data-optanongroupid="C0002"><button ot-accordion="true" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="ot-desc-id-C0002" aria-labelledby="ot-header-id-C0002"></button><!-- Accordion header --><div class="ot-acc-hdr"><div class="ot-plus-minus"><span></span><span></span></div><h4 class="ot-cat-header" id="ot-header-id-C0002">Cookies de desempenho</h4><div class="ot-tgl"><input type="checkbox" name="ot-group-id-C0002" id="ot-group-id-C0002" role="switch" class="category-switch-handler" data-optanongroupid="C0002" checked="" aria-labelledby="ot-header-id-C0002" style=""> <label class="ot-switch" for="ot-group-id-C0002"><span class="ot-switch-nob"></span> <span class="ot-label-txt">Cookies de desempenho</span></label> </div></div><!-- accordion detail --><div class="ot-acc-grpcntr ot-acc-txt"><p class="ot-acc-grpdesc ot-category-desc" id="ot-desc-id-C0002">Estes cookies permitem-nos contar visitas e fontes de tráfego, para que possamos medir e melhorar o desempenho do nosso website. Eles ajudam-nos a saber quais são as páginas mais e menos populares e a ver como os visitantes se movimentam pelo website. Todas as informações recolhidas por estes cookies são agregadas e, por conseguinte, anónimas. Se não permitir estes cookies, não saberemos quando visitou o nosso site.</p></div></div><div class="ot-accordion-layout ot-cat-item ot-vs-config" data-optanongroupid="C0005"><button ot-accordion="true" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="ot-desc-id-C0005" aria-labelledby="ot-header-id-C0005"></button><!-- Accordion header --><div class="ot-acc-hdr"><div class="ot-plus-minus"><span></span><span></span></div><h4 class="ot-cat-header" id="ot-header-id-C0005">Cookies das redes sociais</h4><div class="ot-tgl"><input type="checkbox" name="ot-group-id-C0005" id="ot-group-id-C0005" role="switch" class="category-switch-handler" data-optanongroupid="C0005" checked="" aria-labelledby="ot-header-id-C0005" style=""> <label class="ot-switch" for="ot-group-id-C0005"><span class="ot-switch-nob"></span> <span class="ot-label-txt">Cookies das redes sociais</span></label> </div></div><!-- accordion detail --><div class="ot-acc-grpcntr ot-acc-txt"><p class="ot-acc-grpdesc ot-category-desc" id="ot-desc-id-C0005">Estes cookies são estabelecidos por uma série de serviços das redes sociais que adicionamos ao website para permitir que possa partilhar o nosso conteúdo com os seus amigos e conhecidos. Eles são capazes de rastrear a sua navegação por outros websites e criar um perfil sobre os seus interesses. Isso pode afetar o conteúdo e as mensagens que vê noutros websites que visita. Se não permitir estes cookies, talvez não consiga usar ou ver essas ferramentas de partilha.</p></div></div><div class="ot-accordion-layout ot-cat-item ot-vs-config" data-optanongroupid="C0003"><button ot-accordion="true" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="ot-desc-id-C0003" aria-labelledby="ot-header-id-C0003"></button><!-- Accordion header --><div class="ot-acc-hdr"><div class="ot-plus-minus"><span></span><span></span></div><h4 class="ot-cat-header" id="ot-header-id-C0003">Cookies de publicidade</h4><div class="ot-tgl"><input type="checkbox" name="ot-group-id-C0003" id="ot-group-id-C0003" role="switch" class="category-switch-handler" data-optanongroupid="C0003" checked="" aria-labelledby="ot-header-id-C0003" style=""> <label class="ot-switch" for="ot-group-id-C0003"><span class="ot-switch-nob"></span> <span class="ot-label-txt">Cookies de publicidade</span></label> </div></div><!-- accordion detail --><div class="ot-acc-grpcntr ot-acc-txt"><p class="ot-acc-grpdesc ot-category-desc" id="ot-desc-id-C0003">Estes cookies podem ser estabelecidos através do nosso site pelos nossos parceiros de publicidade. Podem ser usados por essas empresas para construir um perfil sobre os seus interesses e mostrar-lhe anúncios relevantes em outros websites. Eles não armazenam diretamente informações pessoais, mas são baseados na identificação exclusiva do seu navegador e dispositivo de internet. Se não permitir estes cookies, terá menos publicidade direcionada.</p></div></div><!-- Non Accordion Group --><!-- Accordion Group section starts --><!-- Accordion Group section ends --></section></div><section id="ot-pc-lst" class="ot-hide ot-pc-scrollbar"><div id="ot-pc-hdr"><div id="ot-lst-title"><button class="ot-link-btn back-btn-handler" aria-label="Back"><svg id="ot-back-arw" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" x="0px" y="0px" viewBox="0 0 444.531 444.531" xml:space="preserve"><title>Back Button</title><g><path fill="#656565" d="M213.13,222.409L351.88,83.653c7.05-7.043,10.567-15.657,10.567-25.841c0-10.183-3.518-18.793-10.567-25.835
